###############################################################################
# Scars and tattoos
###############################################################################

# lodge.65 Battle scars and lodge tattoos at the start (hidden)
# On a new game (not a save): a scarred character gets the scars shown on
# the portrait, and a landless veteran or Hero of the Warriors of Ulric or
# the Norscan war-bands gets the lodge's tattoos. Scope: every character.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.65
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_save_game = no
		OR = {
			trait = scarred
			trait = scarred_mid
			trait = scarred_high
			lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = scarred trait = scarred_mid trait = scarred_high } }
			add_scarred_start_effect = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes } lodge_tattoo_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.66 Lodge tattoos (hidden)
# Sometimes sent when a veteran finishes a lodge mission: the Warriors of
# Ulric ink the wolf on their veterans and Heroes, the Norscans their own
# marks. Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.66
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		lodge_tattoo_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The clans muster (the Warriors Guild of the Dawi, rank 2)
###############################################################################

# lodge.70 The clans muster (hidden)
# From warrior_lodge_slavic_stand_together: picks a landless brother of
# the guild, brave and of the caller's clan if possible, to bring a
# throng to the war. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.70
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_society_member = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = no
				religion = ROOT
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					host = { character = ROOT }
					any_spouse = { is_ruler = yes }
					prisoner = yes
					any_close_relative = { ai = no }
					host = { ai = no }
					is_heir = ROOT
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					in_command = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
					has_character_flag = no_court_invites
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave trait = stubborn dynasty = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave trait = stubborn }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave dynasty = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave }
			preferred_limit = { martial = 12 }
			save_event_target_as = scoped_slavic_savior
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { always = yes } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.71 }
		}
		else = {
			change_society_currency = 500
			remove_character_modifier = lodge_slavic_stand_together_cooldown
		}
	}
}

# lodge.71 The throng arrives
# A thane of the guild brings a throng of warriors, thunderers and
# hammerers to the capital and takes a commander's post for the war. They
# may grow fond of each other: love (lodge.72) or friendship (lodge.76).
# Scope: the caller; event_target:scoped_slavic_savior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.71
	desc = EVTDESClodge71
	picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_03 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge71
		event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { move_character = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = { has_minor_title = title_commander }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { martial = 10 } }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { martial = 15 } }
				remove_title = title_commander
			}
			event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { give_minor_title = title_commander }
		}
		capital_scope = {
			show_scope_change = no
			ROOT = {
				show_scope_change = no
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					leader = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						pikemen = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						archers = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						gunpowder = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { #Love
					trigger = {
						NOT = { num_of_lovers = 3 }
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { NOT = { num_of_lovers = 3 } }
						opinion = { who = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior value = -20 }
						NOT = { is_close_relative = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior }
						OR = {
							AND = {
								prefers_men_trigger = yes
								event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_female = no }
							}
							AND = {
								prefers_men_trigger = no
								event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_female = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = gregarious }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = shy } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 15 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 20 }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = cruel }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = paranoid }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.75
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.75
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = inbred }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { age = 150 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = strong }
					}
					character_event = { id = lodge.72 days = 30 random = 15 }
				}
				25 = { #Friendship
					trigger = {
						NOT = { num_of_friends = 3 }
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { NOT = { num_of_friends = 3 } }
						opinion = { who = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior value = -20 }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = gregarious }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = shy } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 15 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 20 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						trait = drunkard
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = drunkard }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = paranoid }
					character_event = { id = lodge.76 days = 30 random = 15 }
				}
				50 = { }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.72 Fond of the thane
# Scope: the caller; event_target:scoped_slavic_savior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.72
	desc = EVTDESClodge72
	picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge72
		tooltip = { add_lover = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { character_event = { id = lodge.73 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge72
	}
}

# lodge.73 The thane's answer
# The thane returns the feeling (lodge.74) or does not (lodge.75).
# Scope: the thane; FROM is the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.73
	desc = EVTDESClodge73
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge73
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = yes FROM = { is_female = no } }
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = no FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			}
		}
		add_lover = FROM
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { add_lover = ROOT character_event = { id = lodge.74 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge73
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = yes FROM = { is_female = no } }
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = no FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.75 } } }
	}
}

# lodge.80 The call to war
# From knights_call_of_the_war (rank 3, the warrior lodges and the Chaos
# warbands): the realm's levies answer faster for two years.
# Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.80
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_chaos }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge80_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge80_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge80_dawi }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge80_chaos }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = knights_call_of_the_war_boost duration = 720 }
	}
}

# lodge.81 The knightly oath
# From pledge_to_knighthood (the Knightly Orders, rank 2): swear to one of
# the orders of the Old World. Which orders are open depends on the
# region of the capital and the knight's faith. A knight who finds none
# may wait and ask again. Scope: the knight.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.81
	title = THE_KNIGHTLY_BROTHERHOOD
	desc = EVTDESClodge81
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				culture_group = estalian_group
				culture_group = tilean_group
				culture_group = kislevite_group
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { OR = { culture_group = estalian_group culture_group = tilean_group } }
		picture = GFX_evt_knighs_black_rose
	}
	picture = { trigger = { culture_group = kislevite_group } picture = GFX_evt_battle_kislev_gods }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #The Reiksguard
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_reiksguard
		trigger = { capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_empire_south } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = reiksguard_knight duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.1 OR = { trait = ambitious trait = proud } }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights Panther
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_panther
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = {
				region = world_old_world_empire
				NOT = { region = world_old_world_empire_south }
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_panther duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.75 OR = { trait = cruel trait = wroth } } }
	}
	option = { #The Knights of the Fiery Heart
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_fiery_heart
		trigger = { capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_empire } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_fiery_heart duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.1 OR = { trait = zealous trait = shrewd } }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights of the Blazing Sun
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_blazing_sun
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_empire NOT = { culture = ostlander } }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_blazing_sun duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 OR = { trait = proud trait = zealous } }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights of the Bull
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_bull
		trigger = { capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_empire culture = ostlander } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_bull duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture = ostlander }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights of the Raven
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_raven
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = {
				OR = { region = world_old_world_tilea region = world_old_world_border_princes }
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_raven duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.5 religion = morrian } }
	}
	option = { #The Knights Encarmine
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_encarmine
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = world_old_world_tilea
					region = world_old_world_border_princes
					region = world_old_world_estalia
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_encarmine duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = greedy }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights of Fury
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_fury
		trigger = { capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_estalia } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_fury duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = wroth } }

		name = EVTOPTlodge81_eagle

		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_eagle duration = -1 }

		ai_chance = {

		}

		name = EVTOPTlodge81_black_rose
		trigger = { religion = morrian }

	}
	option = { #The Hammer of Sigmar (Sigmar)
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_hammer_of_sigmar
		trigger = { religion = sigmarite }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_hammer_of_sigmar duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { #Taal's Fury (Taal)
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_taals_fury
		trigger = { religion = taalite }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_taals_fury duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous } }
	}
	option = { #The Knights of the Mariner (Manann)
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_mariner
		trigger = { religion = manannite }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_mariner duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { #The White Wolves (Ulric)
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_white_wolf
		trigger = { religion = ulrican }
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_white_wolf duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous } }
	}
	option = { #The Gryphon Legion (Kislev)
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_gryhpon_legion
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = northern_gods_reformed
				religion = kislev_gods
				religion = northern_gods
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_gryhpon_legion duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous } }
	}
	option = { #Not yet
		name = EVTOPTZlodge81
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_warrior_training
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The young recruit
###############################################################################

# lodge.100 The young recruit (hidden)
# From lodge.12 (a child pushed into the society by a parent passed the
# trial) and after each event of this section, until the child is 20.
# A child who never wanted to fight complains first (lodge.125). Otherwise
# the parent hears that the training crowds out the child's studies, court
# manners, wits or looks, or a Hero of the society offers to teach the
# child courage, fitness or swordplay. About one event a year.
# Scope: the child; event_target:recruiting_parent, martial_society_to_join.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.100
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_young_recruit_trigger = yes
		event_target:recruiting_parent = { same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { trait = incapable } }
	}

	immediate = {
		# First time only: did the child want to fight at all?
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					trait = brave
					trait = rowdy
					has_focus = focus_ch_struggle
					has_focus = focus_ed_martial
					has_focus = focus_war
					has_focus = focus_hunting
					has_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				20 = { # Never wanted this
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = haughty }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = indolent }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = fussy }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = timid }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = feeble }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = sturdy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = robust }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = weak }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = strong }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { martial = 6 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 NOT = { martial = 3 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						is_female = yes
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_equal
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						is_female = no
						has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
					}
					character_event = { id = lodge.125 days = 150 random = 150 }
				}
				80 = {
					set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
					lodge_child_training_pick_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
			lodge_child_training_pick_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# lodge.101 The books gather dust
# Scope: the parent; FROM is the child.

	id = lodge.101
	title = EVTTITLElodge100_training
	desc = EVTDESClodge101
	picture = GFX_evt_child_sword
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { lodge_young_recruit_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge101
		tooltip = { FROM = { change_martial = 1 change_learning = -2 } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.102 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge101
		tooltip = { FROM = { lodge_child_stress_effect = yes } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.103 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

			factor = 20

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.119 Training with a Hero
# Scope: the child; FROM is the parent; event_target:child_hero_mentor.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.119
	title = EVTTITLElodge119
	desc = { trigger = { trait = sturdy } text = EVTDESClodge119_stout }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = feeble } text = EVTDESClodge119_feeble }
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:child_hero_mentor

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge119
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 15 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = sturdy }
			remove_trait = sturdy
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.120 days = 50 } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = feeble }
			remove_trait = feeble
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.121 days = 50 } } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ch_struggle }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ed_martial }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = diligent }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge119
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.122 days = 50 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = indolent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = fussy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = sturdy trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = feeble trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } }
	}
}

# lodge.122 Still soft
# Scope: the parent; FROM is the child; event_target:child_hero_mentor.

	picture = GFX_evt_child_sword
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = event_target:child_hero_mentor

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge122
		if = { limit = { same_society_as = FROM } detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.125 Not a fighter
# The child never wanted this: too lazy, too bookish, too fine for the
# barracks, or too afraid. Scope: the child; event_target:recruiting_parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.125
	title = EVTTITLElodge125
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_lazy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_scholar
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_lady
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_coward
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:recruiting_parent = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = { NOR = { trait = diligent trait = conscientious } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = indolent }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_focus = focus_ed_learning }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = conscientious }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = groomed }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = fussy }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = uncouth }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = timid }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = craven }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge125
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:recruiting_parent = { character_event = { id = lodge.126 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge125
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 25 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

		text = EVTDESClodge126_lazy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_scholar
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_lady
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_coward
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # You will obey
		name = EVTOPTAlodge126
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge126_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.127 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = { # Let the child go
		name = EVTOPTBlodge126
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = stubborn } }
		FROM = {
			leave_society = yes
			set_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband
			lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		}
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.127 The answer is no
# The child gives in (and is miserable), runs away with the treasury or,
# at 16 or older, takes their own life. Scope: the child; FROM is the
# parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.127
	title = EVTTITLElodge125
	desc = EVTDESClodge127
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				is_within_diplo_range = FROM
				NOR = {
					character = ROOT
					character = FROM
					same_society_as = FROM
					same_society_as = ROOT
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { is_rival = FROM }
			preferred_limit = { religion = ROOT culture = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { religion = ROOT }
			save_event_target_as = fleeing_target
		}
	}

	option = { # Give in
		name = EVTOPTAlodge127
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
		add_trait = depressed
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.128 days = 25 } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.100 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { # Run away
		name = EVTOPTBlodge127
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = no
			host = { character = FROM }
			event_target:fleeing_target = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = ROOT value = 0.5 min = 100 max = 250 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.129 } }
			move_character = event_target:fleeing_target
			set_character_flag = no_court_invites
			leave_society = yes
			set_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband
			character_event = { id = lodge.131 days = 450 random = 250 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { # Take my own life
		name = EVTOPTClodge127
		trigger = { age = 16 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.130 } } }
		death = { death_reason = death_suicide }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { # Already miserable: endure anyway
		name = EVTOPTDlodge127
		trigger = { trait = depressed }
		lodge_child_stress_effect = yes
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.128 days = 25 } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.100 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
}

# lodge.131 Whatever happened to the runaway? (hidden)
# Years later the runaway has become what they were afraid of becoming,
# and word reaches the parent (lodge.132 - lodge.135).
# Scope: the runaway; FROMFROM is the parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.131
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_ruler = no
		NOR = { top_liege = { character = FROMFROM } trait = incapable }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = sturdy
			add_trait = slothful
			add_trait = gluttonous
			add_trait = drunkard
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_merry }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_drunkard }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_fat }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.132 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = feeble
			add_trait = erudite
			add_trait = craven
			add_trait = uncouth
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_frail }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_recluse }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_wise }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.133 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = dull
			add_trait = proud
			add_trait = content
			add_trait = groomed
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_frail }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_fair }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_gracious }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.134 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = paranoid
			add_trait = craven
			add_trait = uncouth
			add_trait = shy
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_shadow }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whisperer }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_twitchy }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.135 } }
		}
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Missions

# lodge.200 The missions (hidden)
# Every two years (on_society_bi_yearly_pulse) a Hero of a warrior lodge,
# a warband or the Amazons may set a member a mission: raid a rich
# settlement, take up a warrior's focus, duel a fellow member, duel a
# rival to the death, make war (county conquest, a vassal's or a
# neighbour's lands, the old lands for Ulric, a grudge for the Dawi) or
# bring a child into the fold. The AI mostly settles missions in the
# background. A society whose leader runs its own missions (the flag
# own_mission_tombola: Bretonnia, Kislev, the Dogs of War) is skipped.
# Scope: the member; event_target:lodge_hero (the Hero who asks).
character_event = {
	id = lodge.200
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		any_society_member = {
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			is_society_grandmaster = yes
			same_society_as = ROOT
			NOT = { has_character_flag = own_mission_tombola }
		}
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		# The Hero who asks: a Hero of the society, else a veteran, else the
		# leader.
		random_society_member = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } same_society_as = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { society_rank == 4 }
			preferred_limit = { society_rank == 3 }
			save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = home_capital } # For distance checks
		}

		# Raid (201), focus (204), friendly duel (220), duel to the death
		# (225), war (227, 240, 242, 244, 246), a child (235); the AI often
		# settles the mission in the background.

		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = { #Ruler should be allowed to loot at all in the first place
					is_landed = yes
					is_allowed_to_loot = yes
					OR = { independent = yes top_liege = { is_allowed_to_loot = yes } }
					#A lootable province must exist.
					any_independent_ruler = {
						NOT = { war_with = ROOT }
						NOR = {
							is_liege_of = ROOT
							has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
							is_allied_with = ROOT
							AND = {
								is_tributary = yes
								suzerain = {
									OR = { character = ROOT any_liege = { character = ROOT } }
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								ROOT = {
									OR = {
										religion = norscan_gods
										has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
										is_seafarer = yes
									}
								}
								NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
						}
						any_realm_province = {
							is_occupied = no
							OR = {
								any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
								any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
								ROOT = {
									any_demesne_province = {
										port = yes
										TECH_NAVAL = 1
										OR = {
											has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
											has_building = tb_shipyard_1
											has_building = ca_shipyard_1
											has_building = tp_shipyard_1
											has_building = ct_shipyard_1
										}
										can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
									}
								}
							}
							capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
							holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
							holder_scope = {
								NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
								top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.201 } #Raid and loot barony.
				}
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					is_landed = yes
					NOR = {
						has_focus = focus_war
						has_focus = focus_hunting
						has_focus = focus_rulership
						has_focus = focus_carousing
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.204 } #Lose Weak Focus.
				}
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					age = 16
					society = {
						any_society_member = {
							ai = yes
							age = 16
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
							NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.220 } #Duel <someone>. First blood.
				}
			}
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					ai = no
					age = 16
					OR = {
						AND = {
							any_society_member = {
								NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
								lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
								NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
								is_adult = yes
								ai = yes

							}
						}
						AND = {
							any_rival = {
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no

						}

								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }

								NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }

							}

						}
					}

									target = PREVPREV
									thirdparty_title = PREV
									casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
								}
							}
							location = {
								OR = {
									AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...

											target = event_target:home_capital
											distance < 1000
										}
									}

											target = event_target:home_capital

											target = event_target:home_capital
											distance < 1000
										}

								}
							}
						}
						holder_scope = {
							top_liege = {
								NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
								# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength:
								NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
								relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
							}
						}
					}
				}

										target = PREVPREVPREV

									}

# lodge.201 - lodge.203 Raid and loot

# the mission. Also sent by the Dogs of War missions (DoWA.200).

character_event = {
	id = lodge.201
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.202 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.202
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_skaven }

	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_any }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes

	}

	immediate = {
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { war_with = ROOT }

						suzerain = { OR = { character = ROOT any_liege = { character = ROOT } } }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						ROOT = {
							OR = {
								religion = norscan_gods
								has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
								is_seafarer = yes
							}
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
					}
					NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
				}
				any_realm_province = {
					is_occupied = no
					OR = {
						any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
						any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
						ROOT = {
							any_demesne_province = {
								port = yes
								TECH_NAVAL = 1
								OR = {
									has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
									has_building = tb_shipyard_1
									has_building = ca_shipyard_1
									has_building = tp_shipyard_1
									has_building = ct_shipyard_1
								}
								can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
					capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
					holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					holder_scope = {
						NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
						top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
					}
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			random_realm_province = {
				limit = {
					is_occupied = no
					capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
							any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
							NOT = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes }
						}
						ROOT = {
							any_demesne_province = {
								port = yes
								TECH_NAVAL = 1
								OR = {
									has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
									has_building = tb_shipyard_1
									has_building = ca_shipyard_1
									has_building = tp_shipyard_1
									has_building = ct_shipyard_1
								}
								can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					holder_scope = {
						NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
						top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {

				preferred_limit = {

							}

						}

					}
					capital_holding = {
						holding_type = castle #Or at least not tribes.
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {

							ROOT = {

									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes

# lodge.203 The raid is done (on_loot_settlement)
# Scope: the member; FROM is the looted holding.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.203
	desc = EVTDESClodge203
	picture = GFX_evt_vikings_arriving_oldgods
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		FROM = { is_quest_target_of = ROOT }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge203
		clr_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { holding_type = tribal } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 20 max = 50 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { holding_type = castle } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 50 max = 150 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { OR = { holding_type = city holding_type = temple } } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 100 max = 250 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { destroy_random_building = FROM destroy_random_building = FROM }
	}
}

# lodge.204 - lodge.209 A warrior's focus
# The Hero tells a member who neglects the warrior's life to take up war,
# hunting, rulership or carousing; choosing that focus (on_focus_selected)
# ends the mission. Scope: the Hero (204), then the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.204
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.205 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.205
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_war
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_hunting
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_rulership
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_carousing
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		NOR = {
			has_focus = focus_war
			has_focus = focus_hunting
			has_focus = focus_carousing
			has_focus = focus_rulership
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing }
		}
	}

	quest_target = ROOT

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		ROOT = {
			show_scope_change = no
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_war holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_hunting holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_rulership holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_carousing holder = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = { #For the one who instantly selected the focus, bypassing the mission-trigger
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				flag_renounce_focus_for_war = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_war }
						character_event = { id = lodge.206 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_hunting }
						character_event = { id = lodge.207 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_rulership }
						character_event = { id = lodge.208 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_carousing }
						character_event = { id = lodge.209 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.206
	desc = EVTDESClodge206

# lodge.210 - lodge.211 The raid target is ours (on_new_holder*)
# If the looting target came under the member's (or the liege's) rule, the
# mission ends. Scope: the new holder; FROM is the title.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.210
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot }
			any_liege = {
				lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
				has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot }
			character_event = { id = lodge.211 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_liege = {
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				}
			}
			any_liege = {
				limit = {
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.211 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.211
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
				has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				any_realm_title = { is_quest_target_of = ROOT }
			}
			clr_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		}
	}
}

# lodge.220 - lodge.221 A friendly duel
# The Hero asks the member to spar with a fellow member (or with the Hero).
# Scope: the Hero (220), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.220
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.221 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.221
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_any
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge221_self
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
	}

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { is_landed = yes ai = no }

		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				ai = yes
				age = 16
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { #best case (someone within diplo-range, not sick, not seriously injured already)...
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					is_maimed_trigger = no
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
			}

			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					is_maimed_trigger = no
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
		else_if = { #second best choice (same, but outside of diplo-range)...
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					is_maimed_trigger = no #has a maimed trait
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					is_maimed_trigger = no #has a maimed trait
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
		else_if = { #third best option (now also injured or old folks allowed)...
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:duel_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:duel_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_duel_honor holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.222 - lodge.224 A mission target is gone
# On the target's death (on_death) or when the war became invalid, the
# mission fails with a notice. Also serves the Chaos cult duel quest.
# Scope: the dead target (222), then the member; FROM is the target.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.222
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_quest_target = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
					has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
					has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } #Clears duel mission
		}
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_war
					has_quest = quest_military_war
					has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } # Clears the war mission
		}
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } #Clears recruit mission
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.223
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
				has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
				has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly
			}
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_war }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_war
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_quest = quest_warband_war
				has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				has_quest = quest_military_war
			}
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_recruit
		trigger = {
			has_quest = quest_warband_recruit
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_war }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
			has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
			has_quest = quest_warband_war
			has_quest = quest_military_war
			has_quest = quest_grudge_war
			has_quest = quest_warband_recruit
		}
	}

	notification = yes

	option = { #Alas
		name = ALAS
		character_event = { id = lodge.224 days = 1 } # ACTUAL cleanup
	}
}

character_event = { #*Actual* clean up, because the event above can't handle all the instant stuff
	id = lodge.224
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_war
					has_quest = quest_military_war
					has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		}

		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_duel_honor failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_duel_deadly failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_grudge_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_military_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_recruit failure = yes }
		clr_character_flag = deadly_duel@FROM
	}
}

# lodge.225 - lodge.226 A duel to the death
# The Hero names a champion of a rival society, a rival, a commander of
# the realm or of a neighbour. Scope: the Hero (225), then the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.225
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.226 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.226
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { is_landed = yes ai = no }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge226_rival
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge226_other
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = no } }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = { #Best case... (a society member of *another* warrior lodge, who matches or is below your PCS)
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					top_liege = { any_demesne_title = { lower_tier_than = DUKE } }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					top_liege = { any_demesne_title = { lower_tier_than = DUKE } }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Reduced chance to pick people currently at war (unable to duel)...
					value = 10
					at_war_reduction_score = yes
				}

				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #2nd best case (a rival) - PCS is second prio...
			limit = {
				any_rival = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
				}
			}
			any_rival = {
				limit = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #3rd best case (a realm character) who fits the bill...
			limit = {
				any_realm_character = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					ai = yes
					is_adult = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
				}
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					ai = yes
					is_adult = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #4th case (a *neighbor's* realm character) who fits the bill...
			limit = {
				any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
					any_realm_character = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
			}
			random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
				limit = {
					any_realm_character = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				any_realm_character = {
					limit = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
					score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
						value = 10
						potential_rival_score = yes
					}
					score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
						value = 10
						disliked_target_selection_score = yes
					}
					save_event_target_as = duel_target
					break = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = { # Warrior Lodge members who are without diplomatic range, such as mercenaries
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Reduced chance to pick people currently at war (unable to duel)...
					value = 10
					at_war_reduction_score = yes
				}

				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:duel_target

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		set_character_flag = deadly_duel@event_target:duel_target
		event_target:duel_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_duel_deadly holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.227 - lodge.228 Make war
# The Hero points the member at a county to conquer (pagan county
# conquest; a warband takes the warband war quest).
# Scope: the Hero (227), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.227
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.228 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.228
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		any_independent_ruler = {
			any_realm_title = {
				tier = COUNT
				ROOT = {
					can_use_cb = { #against target
						target = PREVPREV
						thirdparty_title = PREV
						casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
					}
				}
				location = {
					OR = {
						AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
							is_coastal = yes
							can_naval_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = {
							#Or they are accessible via land...
							event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
							is_coastal = no
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
							#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
						}
					}
				}
				holder_scope = {
					top_liege = {
						NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
						# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
						NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
						relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_any
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge228_self
		trigger = { event_target:my_war_target_holder = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
	}

	immediate = {
		#Declare a county conquest against someone
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				any_realm_title = {
					tier = COUNT
					ROOT = {
						can_use_cb = { #against target
							target = PREVPREV
							thirdparty_title = PREV
							casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
								is_coastal = yes
								can_naval_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = {
								#Or they are accessible via land...
								event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
								is_coastal = no
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
								#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = {
						top_liege = {
							NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
							# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
							NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
							relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_realm_title = {
				limit = {
					tier = COUNT
					ROOT = {
						can_use_cb = { #against target
							target = PREVPREV
							thirdparty_title = PREV
							casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
								is_coastal = yes
								can_naval_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = {
								#Or they are accessible via land...
								event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
								is_coastal = no
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
								#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = {
						top_liege = {
							NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
							# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
							NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
							relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = my_war_target
				holder_scope = { top_liege = { save_event_target_as = my_war_target_holder } }
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:my_war_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:my_war_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes } }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_war holder = ROOT }
			}
			else = { set_quest_target = { id = quest_military_war holder = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.229 The war is over
# A Hero of the society writes about the war (won, white peace or lost)
# and the society pays out. Scope: the member; event_target:defender_target.
letter_event = {
	id = lodge.229
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	portrait = event_target:lodge_hero

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_quest = quest_warband_war has_quest = quest_military_war }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { #Find some folks who are impressed by your victory...
				limit = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
				any_society_member = {
					limit = {
						same_society_as = ROOT
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
					}
					count = 3
					score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
						value = 10
						warrior_recruit_score = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = impressed@ROOT
				}
			}

			#Selects someone to congratulate you...
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 4
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 3
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 2
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 1 #This is likely never really ever going to be needed. But still.
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_won
		trigger = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_white_peace
		trigger = { has_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_lost
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#I understand
	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_won
			trigger = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_white_peace
			trigger = { has_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_lost
			trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			won_war@event_target:defender_target = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = impressed@ROOT }
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_conquered_new_land years = 10 }
					clr_character_flag = impressed@ROOT
				}
				add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
			}
			white_peace@event_target:defender_target = { add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes }
			lost_war@event_target:defender_target = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_warband_war
		clr_quest = quest_military_war
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target
		clr_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target
		clr_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target
	}
}

# lodge.230 - lodge.233 The war of a mission ends (on_war_ended_*)
# Scope: the defender; FROM is the attacker (the member).
character_event = {
	id = lodge.230
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
			OR = { has_quest = quest_warband_war has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
		any_realm_title = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
	}

	immediate = {
		#To handle Warrior Lodge mission "Wage War" completion...
		random_realm_title = {
			limit = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
			save_event_target_as = target_title
		}
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = attacker_target }
		save_event_target_as = defender_target

		event_target:attacker_target = {
			set_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target
			character_event = { id = lodge.229 days = 6 } # Hero character states that you completed the mission...
		}
	}
}

# lodge.234 A grudge war ends without victory (on_war_ended_whitepeace,
# _defeat, _invalid): the grudge mission fails. A won grudge war ends it in
# the settle_grudge war. Scope: the defender; FROM is the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.234
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { has_quest = quest_grudge_war }
		is_quest_target_of = FROM
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = defender_target
		FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 6 } }
	}
}

# lodge.235 - lodge.236 Bring a child into the fold
# lodge.12 ends the mission when the child passes the trial.
# Scope: the Hero (235), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.235
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.236 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.236
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		is_landed = yes
		is_adult = yes
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_child = {
			is_child_of = PREV
			is_alive = yes
			is_in_society = no
			age = 12
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband }
			OR = { #AIs should only strive to recruit their main heir
				PREV = { ai = no }
				is_primary_heir = PREV
			}
		}
	}

	desc = EVTDESClodge236

	immediate = {
		random_child = { #Select a child to recruit
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				age = 12
				is_in_society = no
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband }
				OR = { #AIs should only strive to recruit their main heir
					PREV = { ai = no }
					is_primary_heir = PREV
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
			save_event_target_as = recruit_to_be
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:recruit_to_be

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:recruit_to_be = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_recruit holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.240 - lodge.241 Reclaim the old lands (Warriors of Ulric)

							is_coastal = no
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
							#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }

					}

			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = {

		}

									target = PREVPREVPREVPREV
