###############################################################################
# Upkeep: recruits and revival
###############################################################################

# warband.60 Recruits for the warbands
# From on_five_year_pulse (common/on_actions/00_on_actions_warbands.txt).
# An independent AI ruler, two of the realm's lords and one other character
# may join a warband that is empty or short of members (warband.61).
# Scope: the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = warband.60
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_independent = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ai = yes warband_recruit_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = warband.61 }
		}
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				is_landed = yes
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				warband_recruit_trigger = yes
			}
			score_value = { value = 10 warrior_recruit_score = yes }
			count = 2
			character_event = { id = warband.61 }
		}
		any_realm_character = {
			limit = { ai = yes warband_recruit_trigger = yes }
			score_value = { value = 10 warrior_recruit_score = yes }
			count = 1
			character_event = { id = warband.61 }
		}
	}
}

# warband.61 A recruit joins
# The warband of the recruit's god (Undivided worshippers, and Beastmen in
# particular, may choose one god), Gork or Mork by temper (brutal
# or kunnin' traits), or the Skaven Warlords. The first member
# becomes the leader. Rank-4 players hear of it (warband.62).
# Scope: the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = warband.61
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				religion_tzeentch_worshipper_trigger = yes
				can_join_society = warband_of_tzeentch
			}
			join_society = warband_of_tzeentch
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion_nurgle_worshipper_trigger = yes
				can_join_society = warband_of_nurgle
			}
			join_society = warband_of_nurgle
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion_slaanesh_worshipper_trigger = yes
				can_join_society = warband_of_slaanesh
			}
			join_society = warband_of_slaanesh
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion_khorne_worshipper_trigger = yes
				can_join_society = warband_of_khorne
			}
			join_society = warband_of_khorne
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion_chaos_worshipper_trigger = yes can_join_society = warband_of_chaos }
			random_list = {
				25 = { join_society = warband_of_chaos }
				5 = {
					trigger = { trait = creature_beastman can_join_society = warband_of_nurgle }
					join_society = warband_of_nurgle
				}
				3 = {
					trigger = { trait = creature_beastman can_join_society = warband_of_tzeentch }
					join_society = warband_of_tzeentch
				}
				5 = {
					trigger = { trait = creature_beastman can_join_society = warband_of_khorne }
					join_society = warband_of_khorne
				}
				5 = {
					trigger = { trait = creature_beastman can_join_society = warband_of_slaanesh }
					join_society = warband_of_slaanesh
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion_group = greenskin_gods_group
				OR = { can_join_society = warband_gork can_join_society = warband_mork }
			}
			# Gork is brutal but kunnin', Mork is kunnin' but brutal: the
			# recruit's temper decides (warband_gork_weight_trigger etc.).
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = { can_join_society = warband_gork }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = proud }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 martial = 14 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 culture_group = orc_group }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = craven }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = patient }
					join_society = warband_gork
				}
				50 = {
					trigger = { can_join_society = warband_mork }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = deceitful }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = schemer }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = paranoid }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = patient }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = ambitious }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 intrigue = 14 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 culture_group = goblin_group }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 has_magic_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = honest }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = wroth }
					join_society = warband_mork
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { culture_group = skaven_group can_join_society = skaven_warlords_society }
			join_society = skaven_warlords_society
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes }
			apply_random_minor_injury_effect = yes # The initiation was not gentle.
			if = {
				limit = {
					society = { NOT = { any_society_member = { is_society_grandmaster = yes } } }
				}
				set_society_grandmaster = yes
				add_society_currency_massive_effect = yes
			}
			society = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { ai = no society_rank = 4 }
					character_event = { id = warband.62 days = 1 random = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# warband.62 The warband grows
# Scope: a rank-4 player of the warband.
character_event = {
	id = warband.62
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband62_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband62_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband62_chaos }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_orcs }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	notification = yes
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = warband_HF_10052_cooldown }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = warband_HF_10052_cooldown duration = 30 hidden = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EXCELLENT
	}
}

# warband.63 A fallen warband rises again
# From on_society_destroyed (common/on_actions/00_on_actions_warbands.txt).
# A fitting courtier or vassal of the last member (or of their liege)
# takes up the banner and leads it.
# Scope: the last member; FROM is the society.
character_event = {
	id = warband.63
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { this_society_is_a_warband_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = warband_to_revive }
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = { warband_revive_candidate_trigger = yes }
				score_value = {
					value = 10
					warrior_recruit_score = yes
					warband_revival_selection_score = yes
				}
				count = 1
				warband_revive_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			liege = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { warband_revive_candidate_trigger = yes }
					score_value = {
						value = 10
						warrior_recruit_score = yes
						warband_revival_selection_score = yes
					}
					count = 1
					warband_revive_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The war sacrifice (rank 3)
###############################################################################

# warband.70 The war sacrifice
# From warband_war_sacrifice (the warbands but Gork and Mork, and the
# Warriors of Ulric): an offering for victory, in the way of each power.
# Undivided, Slaanesh and the Skaven offer kin, a servant or slave, or
# plunder; Khorne wants a champion's blood in a duel to the death, or
# skulls and plunder; Tzeentch takes kin and pays out as fate decides;
# Nurgle shares his gifts with kin, the champion or a whole province.
# Ulric takes no lives: an oath sworn in blood before the flame, or the
# spoils burned in it. The stronger the offering, the stronger the war
# blessing (lodge_war_sacrifice_2 - 4, five years); ten years must pass
# before the next. Scope: the champion.
character_event = {
	id = warband.70
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband70_ulric }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_khorne_champion }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_nurgle_horde }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_tzeentch_army }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_dynasty_member = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
					is_ruler = no
					is_adult = yes
					host = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_heir = ROOT }
					NOT = { trait = has_nurgles_rot }
				}
				save_event_target_as = scoped_war_sacrifice
			}
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { NOT = { dynasty = ROOT } is_ruler = no is_adult = yes }
				preferred_limit = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes }
				save_event_target_as = sacrificed_courtier
			}
			if = {
				limit = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes }
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { dynasty = ROOT }
						is_ruler = no
						is_adult = yes
						prisoner = no
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						combat_rating >= 50
					}
					preferred_limit = { combat_rating >= 100 }
					save_event_target_as = sacrificed_commander_khorne
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Kin (Undivided, Slaanesh, Skaven)
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband70_chaos }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband70_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband70_skaven }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
			}
			event_target:scoped_war_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_4 years = 5 }
		warband_sacrifice_kin_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
	option = { #A servant or slave (Undivided, Slaanesh, Skaven)
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband70_chaos }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBwarband70_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband70_skaven }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
			}
			event_target:sacrificed_courtier = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_3 years = 5 }
		event_target:sacrificed_courtier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_aztec_sacrifice killer = ROOT }
		}
		warband_sacrifice_butcher_opinion_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #A duel to the death (Khorne)
		name = EVTOPTKwarband70_khorne
		trigger = {
			warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes
			event_target:sacrificed_commander_khorne = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_3 years = 5 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = warband70_duel_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_1
			set_character_flag = deadly_duel@event_target:sacrificed_commander_khorne
			event_target:sacrificed_commander_khorne = {
				save_event_target_as = combatant_2
				apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes
				character_event = { id = HFP.10095 }
			}
			character_event = { id = warband.71 days = 3 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { combat_rating = 50 } } }
	}
	option = { #Kin, and fate decides (Tzeentch)
		name = EVTOPTTwarband70_tzeentch
		trigger = {
			warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes
			event_target:scoped_war_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = warband70_tzeentch_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				33 = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_4 years = 5 } }
				33 = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_3 years = 5 } }
				33 = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_2 years = 10 } }
			}
		}
		warband_sacrifice_kin_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #Share the Grandfather's gifts with kin (Nurgle)
		name = EVTOPTAwarband70_nurgle
		trigger = {
			warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes
			event_target:scoped_war_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_2 years = 5 }
		event_target:scoped_war_sacrifice = {
			show_scope_change = no
			add_trait = has_nurgles_rot
			nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { religion = nurgle } } tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel } }
	}
	option = { #Take the gifts myself (Nurgle)
		name = EVTOPTBwarband70_nurgle
		trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes NOT = { trait = has_nurgles_rot } }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_3 years = 5 }
		add_trait = has_nurgles_rot
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = nurgle_warp value = 100 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { religion = nurgle } } tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOR = { trait = champion_nurgle trait = prince_nurgle trait = chosen_nurgle }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Plague a province (Nurgle)
		name = EVTOPTCwarband70_nurgle
		trigger = {
			warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes
			any_demesne_province = { warband_sacrifice_plague_target_trigger = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_4 years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { capital_scope = { warband_sacrifice_plague_target_trigger = yes } }
			capital_scope = { warband_sacrifice_plague_effect = yes }
		}
		else = {
			random_demesne_province = {
				limit = { warband_sacrifice_plague_target_trigger = yes }
				warband_sacrifice_plague_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { religion = nurgle } } tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Plunder, cattle and skulls (Undivided, Khorne, Slaanesh, Skaven)
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband70_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband70_khorne }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTCwarband70_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband70_skaven }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_2 years = 5 }
		warband_sacrifice_plunder_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #A blood oath at the flame (Ulric)
		name = EVTOPTAwarband70_ulric
		trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_3 years = 5 }
		random = { chance = 25 add_trait_wounded_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = craven }
		}
	}
	option = { #Burn the spoils in the flame (Ulric)
		name = EVTOPTBwarband70_ulric
		trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_2 years = 5 }
		warband_sacrifice_plunder_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.71 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Never mind
		name = EVTOPTZwarband70
		trigger = { ai = no }
		change_society_currency = 600
		clr_character_flag = flag_picking_war_sacrifice
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_picking_war_sacrifice }
			clr_character_flag = flag_picking_war_sacrifice
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = lodge_war_sacrifice_cooldown
				years = 10
				hidden = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# warband.71 The war host sees the offering
# The warriors roar; a Warrior of Ulric or a Norscan veteran may take the
# lodge's tattoos. Scope: the champion.
character_event = {
	id = warband.71
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband71_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { sound_effect = sacrifice_family_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = sacrifice_family_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = sacrifice_family_03 }
		}
		add_blood_splatter_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes lodge_has_tattoo_trigger = no }
			lodge_tattoo_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband71_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband71_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband71_ulric }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# War aid
# Namespace: waraid (was HF.50000 - HF.50107 in hf_warrior_lodge_war_aid_events)
# Faction: Warrior societies
# Serves: the warbands (Chaos, Skaven, Gork and Mork) and the warrior
#   lodges (Warriors of Ulric, the Knightly Orders, the Warriors Guild)
#
# When a war starts, a warband or lodge member on either side may write to
# a player of the same society and ask for help, in the voice of that
# society. If the player turns down the defender and the attacker shares
# the society too, the attacker makes a counter-offer. A player who joins
# becomes the ally's brother in arms (opinion modifier
# waraid_brother_in_arms, which also links the two at the end of the war).
# When the war ends, the ally writes again: glory and society currency for
# a win or a defence held, scorn for a defeat. After a win the ally may
# invite the player to celebrate (a pit fight, a feast, a hunt, a tourney,
# a drinking bout), and that may lead to friendship or love. Rarely, once
# in a life, the ally sends a company of veterans to serve the player.
#
# The call to war              waraid.1 - waraid.3
# The end of the war           waraid.10 - waraid.13
# Celebration and after        waraid.14 - waraid.16
#
# Saved: opinion modifier waraid_brother_in_arms; character flags
#   waraid_asked (5-year cooldown on the player), waraid_spurned_defender,
#   waraid_has_player_ally, waraid_outcome_<won|held|failed|lost>,
#   waraid_got_veterans; earmark waraid_veterans; event targets
#   waraid_defender, waraid_attacker, waraid_player, waraid_ally,
#   waraid_enemy.
#
# Callers: common/on_actions/00_on_actions.txt on_war_started (1),
#   on_war_ended_victory (10), on_war_ended_whitepeace (11),
#   on_war_ended_defeat (12).
# Support: common/scripted_triggers/wh_warband_triggers.txt,
#   common/scripted_effects/wh_warband_effects.txt,
#   common/opinion_modifiers/wh_war_aid_opinions.txt
#
# Fixed from vanilla: the attacker's letters joined the player to the
# defender's side, the attacker could only ask when the defender was a
# member too, the counter-offer could never fire, the letters named event
# targets that were never saved, and the follow-up letter (HF.50106) was
# never sent.
###############################################################################

namespace = waraid

###############################################################################
# The call to war
###############################################################################

# waraid.1 A war starts
# From on_war_started. A warband or lodge member on either side looks for a
# player of the same society to ask for help (waraid.2 or waraid.3).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.1
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes
	war = yes

	trigger = {
		any_war = {
			attacker = { character = FROM }
			defender = { character = ROOT }
			NOT = { using_cb = peasant_revolt }
		}
		OR = { waraid_member_trigger = yes FROM = { waraid_member_trigger = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = waraid_defender
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = waraid_attacker }
		if = {
			limit = {
				waraid_member_trigger = yes
				any_player = { waraid_can_join_trigger = yes same_society_as = ROOT }
			}
			random_player = {
				limit = { waraid_can_join_trigger = yes same_society_as = ROOT }
				save_event_target_as = waraid_player
			}
			event_target:waraid_player = {
				random = {
					chance = 40
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_lover = ROOT }
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_friend = ROOT }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 ROOT = { opinion = { who = PREV value = 30 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_rival = ROOT }
					clr_character_flag = waraid_asked
					set_character_flag = waraid_asked
					letter_event = { id = waraid.2 days = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { waraid_member_trigger = yes }
				any_player = { waraid_can_join_trigger = yes same_society_as = FROM }
			}
			random_player = {
				limit = { waraid_can_join_trigger = yes same_society_as = FROM }
				save_event_target_as = waraid_player
			}
			event_target:waraid_player = {
				random = {
					chance = 30
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_lover = FROM }
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_friend = FROM }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { opinion = { who = PREV value = 30 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_rival = FROM }
					clr_character_flag = waraid_asked
					set_character_flag = waraid_asked
					letter_event = { id = waraid.3 days = 3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# waraid.2 The defender asks for help
# If the player refuses and the attacker shares the society, the attacker
# makes a counter-offer (waraid.3).
# Scope: the player.
letter_event = {
	id = waraid.2
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid2_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid2_any
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		war = no
		event_target:waraid_defender = { war = yes }
		event_target:waraid_attacker = { war = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:waraid_defender = { save_event_target_as = waraid_ally }
		event_target:waraid_attacker = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_any
		}
		join_defender_wars = event_target:waraid_ally
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms years = 10 }
			set_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_any
		}
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:waraid_enemy = { same_society_as = ROOT war = yes }
				NOT = { vassal_of = event_target:waraid_enemy }
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
				letter_event = { id = waraid.3 days = 15 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# waraid.3 The attacker asks for help
# Either the first ask (waraid.1) or a counter-offer after the player turned
# down the defender (waraid.2, flag waraid_spurned_defender).
# Scope: the player.
letter_event = {
	id = waraid.3
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes }
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes }
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3b_any
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender }
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid3_any
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		war = no
		event_target:waraid_attacker = { war = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:waraid_attacker = { save_event_target_as = waraid_ally }
		event_target:waraid_defender = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid2_any
		}
		join_attacker_wars = event_target:waraid_ally
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms years = 10 }
			set_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTBwaraid2_any
		}
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = waraid_spurned_defender
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The end of the war
###############################################################################

# waraid.10 The attacker wins
# From on_war_ended_victory. Each player who fought beside one side hears the
# outcome from their ally (waraid.13).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.10
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
			FROM = { has_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_player = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			FROM = {
				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally
				remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
			set_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won
			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}
		any_player = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			ROOT = {
				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally
				remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
			set_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost
			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally }
	}
}

# waraid.11 White peace
# From on_war_ended_whitepeace. Each player who fought beside one side hears the
# outcome from their ally (waraid.13).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.11
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

		OR = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally

		}

		any_player = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}

				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally
				remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }

			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
			set_character_flag = waraid_outcome_failed
			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}

				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }

				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally

			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally }
	}
}

# waraid.12 The defender wins
# From on_war_ended_defeat. Each player who fought beside one side hears the
# outcome from their ally (waraid.13).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.12
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally

		}

		any_player = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			FROM = {
				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally
				remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
			set_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost
			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}
		any_player = {
			limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			ROOT = {
				save_event_target_as = waraid_ally
				remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = waraid_brother_in_arms }
			}
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = waraid_enemy }
			set_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won
			letter_event = { id = waraid.13 days = 7 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = waraid_has_player_ally }
	}
}

# waraid.13 The ally writes after the war
# Won: glory, perhaps a celebration (waraid.14) or, rarely, veterans
# (waraid.16). Held: some glory. Failed or lost: scorn.
# Scope: the player.
letter_event = {
	id = waraid.13
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won } text = EVTDESCwaraid13_won }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_held } text = EVTDESCwaraid13_held }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_failed }
		text = EVTDESCwaraid13_failed
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost } text = EVTDESCwaraid13_lost }
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Victory! Let them tell the tale.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwaraid13
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won }
		add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		prestige = 100
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = waraid_got_veterans } is_landed = yes }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = waraid.16 days = 20 } }
			}
		}
		random = { chance = 50 hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = waraid.14 days = 30 } } }
	}

	# Is that all? I expected a share of the spoils.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwaraid13
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won }
		add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:waraid_ally = { wealth = 50 } }
			wealth = 50
			event_target:waraid_ally = { show_scope_change = no wealth = -50 }
		}
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
	}

	# We held. That is enough.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwaraid13
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_held }
		add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		prestige = 50
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
		}
		random = { chance = 25 hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = waraid.14 days = 30 } } }
	}

	# The nerve of this brazen fool!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDwaraid13
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_failed
				has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = event_target:waraid_ally modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
	}

	# What should I have done differently?
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEwaraid13
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_failed
				has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost
			}
		}
		prestige = -25
		random = { chance = 25 change_martial = 1 }
	}

	# There is truth in these harsh words. I must abandon my craven ways.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFwaraid13
		trigger = { has_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost trait = craven }
		remove_trait_craven_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = waraid_outcome_won
		clr_character_flag = waraid_outcome_held
		clr_character_flag = waraid_outcome_failed
		clr_character_flag = waraid_outcome_lost
	}
}

# waraid.14 The victory celebration
# Each society celebrates its own way: a pit fight before the altar, a
# Skaven feast (bring your own food), a greenskin brawl, an Ulrican hunt, a
# tourney, a dawi drinking bout. Winning may lead to waraid.15.
# Scope: the player.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.14
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid14_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid14_any
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_orcs }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_hunt }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_joust }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_feast }
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_feast
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:waraid_ally = { is_alive = yes }
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAwaraid14_any
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 waraid_contest_strong_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 waraid_contest_skilled_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_weak_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_disability_trigger = yes }
				custom_tooltip = { text = waraid_contest_won_tt }
				prestige = 100
				add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
				event_target:waraid_ally = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = common_interests_opinion years = 5 }
				}
				hidden_tooltip = {
					opinion = {
						who = event_target:waraid_ally
						modifier = common_interests_opinion
						years = 5
					}
					random = {
						chance = 50
						character_event = { id = waraid.15 days = 90 random = 60 }
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = waraid_contest_lost_tt }
				waraid_contest_lost_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwaraid14
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_very_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# waraid.15 Letters after the celebration
# Restored from vanilla (HF.50106, never sent): the ally keeps writing.
# Scope: the player.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.15
	desc = EVTDESCwaraid15
	picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:waraid_ally = { is_alive = yes }
		NOT = { is_friend = event_target:waraid_ally }
		NOT = { is_lover = event_target:waraid_ally }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwaraid15
		add_friend = event_target:waraid_ally
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwaraid15
		trigger = {
			NOT = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
			event_target:waraid_ally = {
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
				attacted_to_root_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		add_lover = event_target:waraid_ally
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwaraid15
		event_target:waraid_ally = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_very_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# waraid.16 The veterans arrive
# Rarely, once in a life, after a victory the ally sends a company of
# veterans to serve the player for good (half upkeep).
# Scope: the player.
character_event = {
	id = waraid.16
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwaraid16_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCwaraid16_any
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war }
